<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ يَحْيَى بْنِ حَبَّانَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي رَجُلاَنِ، مِنْ أَشْجَعَ أَنَّ مُحَمَّدَ بْنَ مَسْلَمَةَ الأَنْصَارِيَّ، كَانَ يَأْتِيهِمْ مُصَدِّقًا فَيَقُولُ لِرَبِّ الْمَالِ أَخْرِجْ إِلَىَّ صَدَقَةَ مَالِكَ ‏.‏ فَلاَ يَقُودُ إِلَيْهِ شَاةً فِيهَا وَفَاءٌ مِنْ حَقِّهِ إِلاَّ قَبِلَهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ السُّنَّةُ عِنْدَنَا - وَالَّذِي أَدْرَكْتُ عَلَيْهِ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا - أَنَّهُ لاَ يُضَيَّقُ عَلَى الْمُسْلِمِينَ فِي زَكَاتِهِمْ وَأَنْ يُقْبَلَ مِنْهُمْ مَا دَفَعُوا مِنْ أَمْوَالِهِمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/603" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Muhammad ibn Maslama al-Ansari (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Muhammad ibn Yahya ibn Habban said, &quot;Two men from the Ashja tribe told me that Muhammad ibn Maslama al-Ansari used tocome to them to collect their zakat, and he would say to anyone who owned livestock, &#x27;Select (the animal for) the zakat on your livestock and bring it to me,&#x27; and he would accept any sheep that was brought to him provided it met the requirements of what the man owed.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 603]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/603" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 603</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   603 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The sunna with us, and what I have seen the people of knowledge doing in our city, is that things are not made difficult for the muslims in their paying zakat, and whatever they offer of their livestock is accepted from them</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/603" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुहम्मद बिन यह्या बिन हब्बान से रिवायत है के मुझे क़बीला अशजा के दो शख़्सों ने ख़बर दी के मुहम्मद बिन मसलमा अंसारी (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ज़कात लेने आते थे, तो साहिब-ए-माल से कहते थे: लाओ मेरे पास ज़कात अपने माल की। फिर वह जो बकरी ले कर आता, अगर वह ज़कात के लायक़ होती तो क़बूल कर लेते।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/603" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muhammad bin Yahya bin Habban se riwayat hai ke mujhe qabeela Asjha ke do shakhson ne khabar di ke Muhammad bin Maslama Ansari (Radi Allahu Anhu) Zakat lene aate the, toh saheb-e-maal se kehte the: Lao mere paas Zakat apne maal ki. Phir woh jo bakri le kar aata, agar woh Zakat ke laiq hoti toh qabool kar lete.</p>

## Narration 602

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A604)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تَحِلُّ الصَّدَقَةُ لِغَنِيٍّ إِلاَّ لِخَمْسَةٍ لِغَازٍ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ أَوْ لِعَامِلٍ عَلَيْهَا أَوْ لِغَارِمٍ أَوْ لِرَجُلٍ اشْتَرَاهَا بِمَالِهِ أَوْ لِرَجُلٍ لَهُ جَارٌ مِسْكِينٌ فَتُصُدِّقَ عَلَى الْمِسْكِينِ فَأَهْدَى الْمِسْكِينُ لِلْغَنِيِّ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي قَسْمِ الصَّدَقَاتِ أَنَّ ذَلِكَ لاَ يَكُونُ إِلاَّ عَلَى وَجْهِ الاِجْتِهَادِ مِنَ الْوَالِي فَأَىُّ الأَصْنَافِ كَانَتْ فِيهِ الْحَاجَةُ وَالْعَدَدُ أُوثِرَ ذَلِكَ الصِّنْفُ بِقَدْرِ مَا يَرَى الْوَالِي وَعَسَى أَنْ يَنْتَقِلَ ذَلِكَ إِلَى الصِّنْفِ الآخَرِ بَعْدَ عَامٍ أَوْ عَامَيْنِ أَوْ أَعْوَامٍ فَيُؤْثَرُ أَهْلُ الْحَاجَةِ وَالْعَدَدِ حَيْثُمَا كَانَ ذَلِكَ وَعَلَى هَذَا أَدْرَكْتُ مَنْ أَرْضَى مِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِلْمِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلَيْسَ لِلْعَامِلِ عَلَى الصَّدَقَاتِ فَرِيضَةٌ مُسَمَّاةٌ إِلاَّ عَلَى قَدْرِ مَا يَرَى الإِمَامُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 604]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/604" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Zayd ibn Aslam from Ata ibn Yasar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Zakat is not permissible for someone who is not in need except for five:someone fighting in the way of Allah, someone who collects zakat, someone who has suffered (financial) loss (at the hands of debtors), someone who buys it with his own money, and some one who has a poor neighbour who receives some zakat and gives some as a present to the one who is not in need.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The position with us concerning the dividing up of zakat is that it is up to the individual judgement of the man in charge (wali). Whichever categories of people are in most need and are most numerous are given preference, according to how the man in charge sees fit. It is possible that that may change after one year, or two, or more, but it is always those who are in need and are most numerous that are given preference, whatever category they may belong to. This is what I have seen done by people of knowledge with which I am satisifed.&quot; Malik said, &quot;There is no fixed share for the collector of the zakat, except according to what the imam sees fit</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 604]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/604" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अता बिन यसार से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मालदार की ज़कात दुरुस्त नहीं है मगर पाँच आदमियों को दुरुस्त है: पहले ग़ाज़ी जो जिहाद करता हो अल्लाह की राह में, दूसरे वह आमिल जो ज़कात वसूल और तहसील करता हो, तीसरे मद्यून (क़र्ज़दार), चौथे वह जो ज़कात को ख़रीद ले अपने माल के एवाज़ में, पाँचवें वह मिस्कीन जो हमसाये के पास से बतौरे-हदिया के आए।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 604]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/604" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عطاء بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا زکوة درست نہیں مالدار کی مگر پانچ آدمیوں کو درست ہے پہلے غازی جو جہاد کرتا ہو اللہ کی راہ میں دوسرے جو عامل ہو زکوة کا یعنی زکوة کو وصول اور تحصیل کرتا ہو تیسرے مدیون یعنی جو قرضدار ہو چوتھے جو زکوة کو خرید لے اپنے مال کے عوض میں پانچویں جو مسکین ہمسایہ کے پاس سے بطور ہدیہ کے آئے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 604]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/604" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ata bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Maaldaar ki Zakat durust nahi hai magar paanch aadmiyon ko durust hai: Pehle ghazi jo jihad karta ho Allah ki raah mein, dusre woh aamil jo Zakat wasool aur tehseel karta ho, teesre madyoon (qarzdaar), chauthe woh jo Zakat ko khareed le apne maal ke ewaz mein, paanchvein woh miskeen jo hamsaye ke paas se bator-e-hadiya ke aaye.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 604]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/604" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 604

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A606)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 606]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 606</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   606 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ شَرِبَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ لَبَنًا فَأَعْجَبَهُ فَسَأَلَ الَّذِي سَقَاهُ مِنْ أَيْنَ هَذَا اللَّبَنُ فَأَخْبَرَهُ أَنَّهُ وَرَدَ عَلَى مَاءٍ - قَدْ سَمَّاهُ - فَإِذَا نَعَمٌ مِنْ نَعَمِ الصَّدَقَةِ وَهُمْ يَسْقُونَ فَحَلَبُوا لِي مِنْ أَلْبَانِهَا فَجَعَلْتُهُ فِي سِقَائِي فَهُوَ هَذَا ‏.‏ فَأَدْخَلَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ يَدَهُ فَاسْتَقَاءَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ كُلَّ مَنْ مَنَعَ فَرِيضَةً مِنْ فَرَائِضِ اللَّهِ عَزَّ وَجَلَّ فَلَمْ يَسْتَطِعِ الْمُسْلِمُونَ أَخْذَهَا كَانَ حَقًّا عَلَيْهِمْ جِهَادُهُ حَتَّى يَأْخُذُوهَا مِنْهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Zayd ibn Aslam said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al Khattab drank some milk which he liked (very much) and he asked the man who had given it to him, &#x27;Where did this milk come from?&#x27; The man told him that he had come to a watering-place, which he named, and had found grazing livestock from the zakat watering there. He was given some of their milk, which he then put into his water-skin, and that was the milk in question. Umar ibn al-Khattab then put his hand into his mouth to make himself vomit.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 606]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 606</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   606 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The position with us is that if anyone refuses to honour one of the obligatory demands of Allah, and the muslims are unable to get it, then they have the right to fight him until they get itfrom him</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 606]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 606</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   606 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से रिवायत किया के मालिक ने ज़ैद इब्न असलम से सुना के हज़रत उमर इब्न अल-ख़त्ताब रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु ने कुछ दूध पिया जो उन्हें बहुत पसंद आया, तो उन्होंने उस शख़्स से पूछा जिस ने यह दूध दिया था के यह कहाँ से आया है? उस शख़्स ने उन्हें बताया के वह एक चरागाह पर गया था, जिस का उस ने नाम लिया, और वहाँ ज़कात के मवेशियों को चरते हुए पाया। उसे उन का कुछ दूध दिया गया, जो उस ने अपनी मशकीज़े में डाल लिया, और वही यह दूध है। तब हज़रत उमर इब्न अल-ख़त्ताब रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु ने अपने मुँह में हाथ डाला ताकि क़य कर सकें। मालिक ने कहा, &#x27;हमारे नज़दीक उसूल यह है के अगर कोई अल्लाह के वाजिब हुक़ूक़ में से किसी एक का एहतराम करने से इनकार करे और मुसलमान उसे हासिल करने से क़ासिर हों, तो उन्हें हक़ हासिल है के उस के ख़िलाफ़ लड़ें यहाँ तक के वह उसे हासिल कर लें।&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 606</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   606 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے روایت کیا کہ مالک نے زید بن اسلم سے سنا کہ حضرت عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ عنہ نے کچھ دودھ پیا جو انہیں بہت پسند آیا، تو انہوں نے اس شخص سے پوچھا جس نے یہ دودھ دیا تھا کہ یہ کہاں سے آیا ہے؟ اس شخص نے انہیں بتایا کہ وہ ایک چراگاہ پر گیا تھا، جس کا اس نے نام لیا، اور وہاں زکوٰۃ کے مویشیوں کو چرتے ہوئے پایا۔ اسے ان کا کچھ دودھ دیا گیا، جو اس نے اپنی مشکیزے میں ڈال لیا، اور وہی یہ دودھ ہے۔ تب حضرت عمر بن خطاب رضی اللہ عنہ نے اپنے منہ میں ہاتھ ڈالا تاکہ قے کر سکیں۔ مالک نے کہا، &#x27;ہمارے نزدیک اصول یہ ہے کہ اگر کوئی اللہ کے واجب حقوق میں سے کسی ایک کا احترام کرنے سے انکار کرے اور مسلمان اسے حاصل کرنے سے قاصر ہوں، تو انہیں حق حاصل ہے کہ اس کے خلاف لڑیں یہاں تک کہ وہ اسے حاصل کر لیں۔&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 606</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   606 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se riwayat kiya ke Malik ne Zayd ibn Aslam se suna ke Hazrat Umar ibn al-Khattab Razi Allahu Anhu ne kuch doodh piya jo unhein bohat pasand aaya, to unhon ne us shakhs se poocha jis ne yeh doodh diya tha ke yeh kahan se aaya hai? Us shakhs ne unhein bataya ke woh ek charagah par gaya tha, jis ka us ne naam liya, aur wahan zakat ke maweshiyon ko charte hue paya. Use un ka kuch doodh diya gaya, jo us ne apni mashkize mein daal liya, aur wahi yeh doodh hai. Tab Hazrat Umar ibn al-Khattab Razi Allahu Anhu ne apne munh mein haath dala taake qay kar sakein. Malik ne kaha, &#x27;Hamare nazdeek usool yeh hai ke agar koi Allah ke wajib huqooq mein se kisi ek ka ehtram karne se inkar kare aur Musalman use hasil karne se qasir hon, to unhein haq hasil hai ke us ke khilaf ladein yahan tak ke woh use hasil kar lein.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/606" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 605

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A607)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 607]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 607</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   607 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَامِلاً، لِعُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ كَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ يَذْكُرُ أَنَّ رَجُلاً مَنَعَ زَكَاةَ مَالِهِ فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ أَنْ دَعْهُ وَلاَ تَأْخُذْ مِنْهُ زَكَاةً مَعَ الْمُسْلِمِينَ قَالَ فَبَلَغَ ذَلِكَ الرَّجُلَ فَاشْتَدَّ عَلَيْهِ وَأَدَّى بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ زَكَاةَ مَالِهِ فَكَتَبَ عَامِلُ عُمَرَ إِلَيْهِ يَذْكُرُ لَهُ ذَلِكَ فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ أَنْ خُذْهَا مِنْهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-&#x27;Aziz (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that one of the administrators of Umar ibn Abd al-&#x27;Aziz wrote to him mentioning that a man had refused to pay zakat on his property. Umar wrote to the administrator and told him to leave the man alone and not to take any zakat from him when he took it from the other muslims. The man heard about this and the situation became unbearable for him, and after that he paid the zakat on his property. The administrator wrote to Umar and mentioned that to him, and Umar wrote back telling him to take the zakat from him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 607]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 607]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 607</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   607 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलम से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर बिन ख़त्ताब (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने दूध पिया तो भला मालूम हुआ। पूछा के ये दूध कहाँ से आया? जो लाया था वह बोला के मैं एक पानी पर गया था (नाम बयान किया), वहाँ पर जानवर ज़कात के पानी पी रहे थे, लोगों ने उनका दूध निचोड़ कर मुझे दिया, मैंने अपनी मश्क में रख लिया, वह यही दूध था जो आप ने पिया। तो हज़रत उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने अपना हाथ मुँह में डाल कर क़ई (vomit) की। इमाम मालिक को पहुँचा के एक आमिल ने उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को लिखा के एक शख़्स अपने माल की ज़कात नहीं देता। उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने जवाब में लिखा के छोड़ दे उसे और मुसलमानों के साथ ज़कात न लिया कर उस से। ये ख़बर उस शख़्स को पहुँची तो उसे बुरा मालूम हुआ और अपने माल की ज़कात अदा कर दी। बाद इसके आमिल ने हज़रत उमर (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को इत्तला दी, उन्होंने जवाब में लिखा के अब उस शख़्स से ज़कात ले लो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمربن خطاب نے دودھ پیا تو بھلا معلوم ہوا پوچھا کہ یہ دودھ کہاں سے آیا جو لایا تھا وہ بولا کہ میں ایک پانی پر گیا تھا اور اس کا نام بیان کیا وہاں پر جانور زکوة کے پانی پی رہے تھے لوگوں نے ان کا دودھ نچوڑ کر مجھے دیا میں نے اپنی مشک میں رکھ لیا وہ یہی دودھ تھا جو آپ نے پیا تو حضرت عمر نے اپنا ہاتھ منہ میں ڈال کر قے کی ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ ایک عامل نے عمر بن عبدالعزیز کو لکھا کہ ایک شخص اپنے مال کی زکوة نہیں دیتا عمر نے جواب میں لکھا کہ چھوڑ دے اس کو اور مسلمانوں کے ساتھ اور زکوة نہ لیا کر اس سے۔ یہ خبر اس شخص کو پہنچی اس کو برا معلوم ہوا اور اپنے مال کی زکوة ادا کر دی بعد اس کے عامل نے حضرت عمر کو اطلاع دی انہوں نے جواب میں لکھا کہ لے لے زکوة کو اس شخص سے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 607]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 607</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   607 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne doodh piya toh bhala maloom hua. Poocha ke ye doodh kahan se aaya? Jo laya tha woh bola ke main ek paani par gaya tha (naam bayan kiya), wahan par janwar Zakat ke paani pee rahe the, logon ne unka doodh nichod kar mujhe diya, maine apni mashk mein rakh liya, woh yahi doodh tha jo aap ne piya. Toh Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne apna haath munh mein daal kar qai (vomit) ki. Imam Malik ko pahuncha ke ek aamil ne Umar bin Abdul Aziz (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko likha ke ek shakhs apne maal ki Zakat nahi deta. Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne jawab mein likha ke chhod de usay aur musalmanon ke sath Zakat na liya kar us se. Ye khabar us shakhs ko pahunchi toh usay bura maloom hua aur apne maal ki Zakat ada kar di. Baad iske aamil ne Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko ittila di, unhon ne jawab mein likha ke ab us shakhs se Zakat le lo.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/607" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 606

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A608)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ الثِّقَةِ، عِنْدَهُ عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، وَعَنْ بُسْرِ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ فِيمَا سَقَتِ السَّمَاءُ وَالْعُيُونُ وَالْبَعْلِ الْعُشْرُ وَفِيمَا سُقِيَ بِالنَّضْحِ نِصْفُ الْعُشْرِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 608]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/608" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from a reliable source from Sulayman ibn Yasar and from Busr ibn Said that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;On land that is watered by rain or springs or any natural means there is (zakat to pay of) a tenth. On irrigated land there is (zakat of) a twentieth (to pay)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 608]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/608" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार और बुस्र बिन सईद से रिवायत है के आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: बारानी (बारिश की) और ज़ेर-ए-चश्मा या तालाब की ज़मीन में, और उस खजूर में जिसे पानी की हाजत न हो, दसवां हिस्सा (उश्र) ज़कात है, और जो ज़मीन पानी सींच कर तर की जाए उस में बीसवां हिस्सा ज़कात का है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 608]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/608" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار اور بسر بن سعید سے روایت ہے کہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ بارانی اور زیر چشمہ یا تالاب کی زمین میں اور اس کھجور میں جس کو پانی کی حاجت نہ ہو دسواں حصہ زکوۃ ہے اور جو زمین پانی سینچ کر ترکی جائے اس میں بیسواں حصہ زکوة کا ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 608]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar aur Busr bin Saeed se riwayat hai ke Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Barani (baarish ki) aur zer-e-chashma ya talaab ki zameen mein, aur us khajoor mein jise paani ki haajat na ho, daswan hissa (ushr) Zakat hai, aur jo zameen paani seench kar tar ki jaye us mein beeswan hissa Zakat ka hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 608]</p>

## Narration 607

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A609)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 609]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/609" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 609</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   609 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زِيَادِ بْنِ سَعْدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ لاَ يُؤْخَذُ فِي صَدَقَةِ النَّخْلِ الْجُعْرُورُ وَلاَ مُصْرَانُ الْفَارَةِ وَلاَ عَذْقُ ابْنِ حُبَيْقٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَهُوَ يُعَدُّ عَلَى صَاحِبِ الْمَالِ وَلاَ يُؤْخَذُ مِنْهُ فِي الصَّدَقَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا مِثْلُ ذَلِكَ الْغَنَمُ تُعَدُّ عَلَى صَاحِبِهَا بِسِخَالِهَا وَالسَّخْلُ لاَ يُؤْخَذُ مِنْهُ فِي الصَّدَقَةِ وَقَدْ يَكُونُ فِي الأَمْوَالِ ثِمَارٌ لاَ تُؤْخَذُ الصَّدَقَةُ مِنْهَا مِنْ ذَلِكَ الْبُرْدِيُّ وَمَا أَشْبَهَهُ لاَ يُؤْخَذُ مِنْ أَدْنَاهُ كَمَا لاَ يُؤْخَذُ مِنْ خِيَارِهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ وَإِنَّمَا تُؤْخَذُ الصَّدَقَةُ مِنْ أَوْسَاطِ الْمَالِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّهُ لاَ يُخْرَصُ مِنَ الثِّمَارِ إِلاَّ النَّخِيلُ وَالأَعْنَابُ فَإِنَّ ذَلِكَ يُخْرَصُ حِينَ يَبْدُو صَلاَحُهُ وَيَحِلُّ بَيْعُهُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّ ثَمَرَ النَّخِيلِ وَالأَعْنَابِ يُؤْكَلُ رُطَبًا وَعِنَبًا فَيُخْرَصُ عَلَى أَهْلِهِ لِلتَّوْسِعَةِ عَلَى النَّاسِ وَلِئَلاَّ يَكُونَ عَلَى أَحَدٍ فِي ذَلِكَ ضِيقٌ فَيُخْرَصُ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهِمْ ثُمَّ يُخَلَّى بَيْنَهُمْ وَبَيْنَهُ يَأْكُلُونَهُ كَيْفَ شَاءُوا ثُمَّ يُؤَدُّونَ مِنْهُ الزَّكَاةَ عَلَى مَا خُرِصَ عَلَيْهِمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَأَمَّا مَا لاَ يُؤْكَلُ رَطْبًا وَإِنَّمَا يُؤْكَلُ بَعْدَ حَصَادِهِ مِنَ الْحُبُوبِ كُلِّهَا فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يُخْرَصُ وَإِنَّمَا عَلَى أَهْلِهَا فِيهَا إِذَا حَصَدُوهَا وَدَقُّوهَا وَطَيَّبُوهَا وَخَلُصَتْ حَبًّا فَإِنَّمَا عَلَى أَهْلِهَا فِيهَا الأَمَانَةُ يُؤَدُّونَ زَكَاتَهَا إِذَا بَلَغَ ذَلِكَ مَا تَجِبُ فِيهِ الزَّكَاةُ وَهَذَا الأَمْرُ الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا أَنَّ النَّخْلَ يُخْرَصُ عَلَى أَهْلِهَا وَثَمَرُهَا فِي رُءُوسِهَا إِذَا طَابَ وَحَلَّ بَيْعُهُ وَيُؤْخَذُ مِنْهُ صَدَقَتُهُ تَمْرًا عِنْدَ الْجِدَادِ فَإِنْ أَصَابَتِ الثَّمَرَةَ جَائِحَةٌ بَعْدَ أَنْ تُخْرَصَ عَلَى أَهْلِهَا وَقَبْلَ أَنْ تُجَذَّ فَأَحَاطَتِ الْجَائِحَةُ بِالثَّمَرِ كُلِّهِ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِمْ صَدَقَةٌ فَإِنْ بَقِيَ مِنَ الثَّمَرِ شَىْءٌ يَبْلُغُ خَمْسَةَ أَوْسُقٍ فَصَاعِدًا بِصَاعِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أُخِذَ مِنْهُمْ زَكَاتُهُ وَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِمْ فِيمَا أَصَابَتِ الْجَائِحَةُ زَكَاةٌ وَكَذَلِكَ الْعَمَلُ فِي الْكَرْمِ أَيْضًا وَإِذَا كَانَ لِرَجُلٍ قِطَعُ أَمْوَالٍ مُتَفَرِّقَةٌ أَوِ اشْتِرَاكٌ فِي أَمْوَالٍ مُتَفَرِّقَةٍ لاَ يَبْلُغُ مَالُ كُلِّ شَرِيكٍ أَوْ قِطَعُهُ مَا تَجِبُ فِيهِ الزَّكَاةُ وَكَانَتْ إِذَا جُمِعَ بَعْضُ ذَلِكَ إِلَى بَعْضٍ يَبْلُغَ مَا تَجِبُ فِيهِ الزَّكَاةُ فَإِنَّهُ يَجْمَعُهَا وَيُؤَدِّي زَكَاتَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/609" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ziyad ibn Sad that Ibn Shihab said, &quot;Neither jurur, nor musran al-fara, nor adhq ibn hubayq should be taken as zakat from dates. They should be included in the assessment but not taken as zakat. &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 609]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/609" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 609</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   609 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;This is the same as with sheep and goats, whose young are included in the assessment but are not (actually) taken as zakat. There are also certain kinds of fruit which are not taken as zakat, such as burdi dates (one of the finest kinds of dates), and similar varieties. Neither the lowest quality (of any property) nor the highest should be taken. Rather, zakat should be taken from average quality property.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The position that we are agreed upon concerning fruit is that only dates and grapes are estimated while on the tree. They are estimated when their usability is clear and they are halal to sell. This is because the fruit of date-palms and vines is eaten straightaway in the form of fresh dates and grapes, and so the assessment is done by estimation to make things easier for people and to avoid causing them trouble. Their produce is estimated and then they are given a free hand in using their produce as they wish, and later they pay the zakat on it according to the estimation that was made.&quot; Malik said, &quot;crops which are not eaten fresh, such as grains and seeds, which are only eaten after they have been harvested, are not estimated. The owner, after he has harvested, threshed and sifted the crop, so that it is then in the form of grain or seed, has to fulfil his trust himself and deduct the zakat he owes if the amount is large enough for him to have to pay zakat. This is the position that we are all agreed upon here (in Madina).&quot; Malik said, &quot;The position that we are all agreed upon here (in Madina) is that the produce of date palms is estimated while it is still on the tree, after it has ripened and become halal to sell, and the zakat on it is deducted in the form of dried dates at the time of harvest. If the fruit is damaged after it has been estimated and the damage affects all the fruit then no zakat has to be paid. If some of the fruit remains unaffected, and this fruit amounts to five awsuq or more using the sa of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, then zakat is deducted from it. Zakat does not have to be paid, however, on the fruit that was damaged . Grapevines are dealt with in the same way. If a man owns various pieces of property in various places, or is a co-owner of various pieces of property in various places, none of which individually comes to a zakatable amount, but which, when added together, do come to a zakatable amount, then he adds them together and pays the zakat that is due on them</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/609" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शाहब ज़ुहरी ने कहा के खजूर की ज़कात में जा&#x27;रूर (एक क़िस्म की ख़राब खजूर जो सूखने से कूड़ा हो जाती है) और मिश्रण आल-फराह और आज़क़ बिन हाबिक़ (ये भी रद्दी खजूरों की किस्में हैं) न ली जाएँगी. इनकी मिसाल बकरियों की सी है के साहेब-इ-माल के माल के षुमार में सब क़िस्म की षुमार की जाएँगी लेकिन (वसूली में) ली न जाएँगी.</p>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب زہری نے کہا کہ کھجور کی زکوۃ میں جعرور (ایک قسم کی خراب کھجور ہے جو سوکھنے سے کوڑا ہو جاتی ہے) اور مصران الفارہ اور عذق بن حبیق(یہ بھی ردی کھجوروں کی قسم ہیں) نہ لی جائیں گی اور مثال ان کی بکریوں کی سی ہے کہ صاحب مال کے مال کے شمار میں سب قسم کی شمار کی جائیں گی لیکن لی نہ جائیں گی ۔</p>

<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Shahab Zuhri ne kaha ke khajoor ki Zakat mein Ja&#x27;roor (ek qisam ki kharab khajoor jo sookhne se kooda ho jati hai) aur Misran al-Farah aur Azq bin Habiq (ye bhi raddi khajooron ki qismein hain) na li jayengi. Inki misaal bakriyon ki si hai ke saheb-e-maal ke maal ke shumar mein sab qisam ki shumar ki jayengi lekin (wasooli mein) li na jayengi.</p>

## Narration 608

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A610)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 610]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/610" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 610</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   610 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنِ الزَّيْتُونِ، فَقَالَ فِيهِ الْعُشْرُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِنَّمَا يُؤْخَذُ مِنَ الزَّيْتُونِ الْعُشْرُ بَعْدَ أَنْ يُعْصَرَ وَيَبْلُغَ زَيْتُونُهُ خَمْسَةَ أَوْسُقٍ فَمَا لَمْ يَبْلُغْ زَيْتُونُهُ خَمْسَةَ أَوْسُقٍ فَلاَ زَكَاةَ فِيهِ وَالزَّيْتُونُ بِمَنْزِلَةِ النَّخِيلِ مَا كَانَ مِنْهُ سَقَتْهُ السَّمَاءُ وَالْعُيُونُ أَوْ كَانَ بَعْلاً فَفِيهِ الْعُشْرُ وَمَا كَانَ يُسْقَى بِالنَّضْحِ فَفِيهِ نِصْفُ الْعُشْرِ وَلاَ يُخْرَصُ شَىْءٌ مِنَ الزَّيْتُونِ فِي شَجَرِهِ ‏.‏ وَالسُّنَّةُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الْحُبُوبِ الَّتِي يَدَّخِرُهَا النَّاسُ وَيَأْكُلُونَهَا أَنَّهُ يُؤْخَذُ مِمَّا سَقَتْهُ السَّمَاءُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ وَمَا سَقَتْهُ الْعُيُونُ وَمَا كَانَ بَعْلاً الْعُشْرُ وَمَا سُقِيَ بِالنَّضْحِ نِصْفُ الْعُشْرِ إِذَا بَلَغَ ذَلِكَ خَمْسَةَ أَوْسُقٍ بِالصَّاعِ الأَوَّلِ صَاعِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَمَا زَادَ عَلَى خَمْسَةِ أَوْسُقٍ فَفِيهِ الزَّكَاةُ بِحِسَابِ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْحُبُوبُ الَّتِي فِيهَا الزَّكَاةُ الْحِنْطَةُ وَالشَّعِيرُ وَالسُّلْتُ وَالذُّرَةُ وَالدُّخْنُ وَالأُرْزُ وَالْعَدَسُ وَالْجُلْبَانُ وَاللُّوبِيَا وَالْجُلْجُلاَنُ وَمَا أَشْبَهَ ذَلِكَ مِنَ الْحُبُوبِ الَّتِي تَصِيرُ طَعَامًا فَالزَّكَاةُ تُؤْخَذُ مِنْهَا بَعْدَ أَنْ تُحْصَدَ وَتَصِيرَ حَبًّا ‏.‏ قَالَ وَالنَّاسُ مُصَدَّقُونَ فِي ذَلِكَ وَيُقْبَلُ مِنْهُمْ فِي ذَلِكَ مَا دَفَعُوا ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ مَتَى يُخْرَجُ مِنَ الزَّيْتُونِ الْعُشْرُ أَوْ نِصْفُهُ أَقَبْلَ النَّفَقَةِ أَمْ بَعْدَهَا فَقَالَ لاَ يُنْظَرُ إِلَى النَّفَقَةِ وَلَكِنْ يُسْأَلُ عَنْهُ أَهْلُهُ كَمَا يُسْأَلُ أَهْلُ الطَّعَامِ عَنِ الطَّعَامِ وَيُصَدَّقُونَ بِمَا قَالُوا فَمَنْ رُفِعَ مِنْ زَيْتُونِهِ خَمْسَةُ أَوْسُقٍ فَصَاعِدًا أُخِذَ مِنْ زَيْتِهِ الْعُشْرُ بَعْدَ أَنْ يُعْصَرَ وَمَنْ لَمْ يُرْفَعْ مِنْ زَيْتُونِهِ خَمْسَةُ أَوْسُقٍ لَمْ تَجِبْ عَلَيْهِ فِي زَيْتِهِ الزَّكَاةُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمَنْ بَاعَ زَرْعَهُ وَقَدْ صَلَحَ وَيَبِسَ فِي أَكْمَامِهِ فَعَلَيْهِ زَكَاتُهُ وَلَيْسَ عَلَى الَّذِي اشْتَرَاهُ زَكَاةٌ وَلاَ يَصْلُحُ بَيْعُ الزَّرْعِ حَتَّى يَيْبَسَ فِي أَكْمَامِهِ وَيَسْتَغْنِيَ عَنِ الْمَاءِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي قَوْلِ اللَّهِ تَعَالَى ‏{‏وَآتُوا حَقَّهُ يَوْمَ حَصَادِهِ ‏}‏ أَنَّ ذَلِكَ الزَّكَاةُ وَقَدْ سَمِعْتُ مَنْ يَقُولُ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمَنْ بَاعَ أَصْلَ حَائِطِهِ أَوْ أَرْضَهُ وَفِي ذَلِكَ زَرْعٌ أَوْ ثَمَرٌ لَمْ يَبْدُ صَلاَحُهُ فَزَكَاةُ ذَلِكَ عَلَى الْمُبْتَاعِ وَإِنْ كَانَ قَدْ طَابَ وَحَلَّ بَيْعُهُ فَزَكَاةُ ذَلِكَ عَلَى الْبَائِعِ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَشْتَرِطَهَا عَلَى الْمُبْتَاعِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/610" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he asked Ibn Shihab about olives and he said, &quot;There is a tenth on them.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 610]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/610" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 610</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   610 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The tenth that is taken from olives is taken after they have been pressed, and the olives must come to a minimum amount of five awsuq and there must be at least five awsuq of olives. If there are less than five awsuq of olives, no zakat has to be paid. Olive trees are like date palms insofar as there is a tenth on whatever is watered by rain or springs or any natural means, and a twentieth on whatever is irrigated. However, olives are not estimated while on the tree. The sunna with us as far as grain and seeds which people store and eat is concerned is that a tenth is taken from whatever has been watered by rain or springs or any natural means, and a twentieth from whatever has been irrigated, that is, as long as the amount comes to five awsuq or more using the aforementioned sa, that is, the sa of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Zakat must be paid on anything above five awsuq according to the amount involved.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The kinds of grain and seeds on which there is zakat are:wheat, barley, sult (a kind of barley), sorghum, pearl millet, rice, lentils, peas, beans, sesame seeds and other such grains and seeds which are used for food. Zakat is taken from them after they have been harvested and are in the form of grai n or seed.&quot; He said, &quot;People are entrusted with the assessment and whatever they hand over is accepted .&quot; Malik was asked whether the tenth or the twentieth was taken out of olives before they were sold or after and he said, &quot;The sale is not taken into consideration. It is the people who produce the olives that are asked about the olives, just as it is the people who produce foodstuffs that are asked about it, and zakat is taken from them by what they say. Someone who gets five awsuq or more of olives from his olive trees has a tenth taken from the oil after pressing. Whereas someone who does not get five awsuq from his trees does not have to pay any zakat on the oil.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Someone who sells his crops when they are ripe and are ready in the husk has to pay zakat on them but the one who buys them does not. The sale of crops is not valid until they are ready in the husk and no longer need water.&quot; Malik said, concerning the word of Allah the Exalted, &quot;And give its due on the day of its harvesting,&quot; that it referred to zakat, and that he had heard people saying that. Malik said, &quot;If someone sells his garden or his land, on which are crops or fruit which have not yet ripened, then it is the buyer who has to pay the zakat. If, however, they have ripened, it is the seller who has to pay the zakat, unless paying the zakat is one of the conditions of the sale</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/610" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

## Narration 609

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A611)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، عَنْ عِرَاكِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ لَيْسَ عَلَى الْمُسْلِمِ فِي عَبْدِهِ وَلاَ فِي فَرَسِهِ صَدَقَةٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 611]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/611" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 611</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   611 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, &quot;If a man has four awsuq of dates he has harvested, four awsuq of grapes he has picked, or four awsuq of wheat he has reaped or four awsuq of pulses he has harvested, the different categories are not added together, and he does not have to pay zakat on any of the categ ries - the dates, the grapes, the wheat or the pulses - until any one of them comes to five awsuq using the sa of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, as the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;There is no zakat (to pay) on anything less than five awsuq of dates. &#x27;lf any of the categories comes to five awsuq, then zakat must be paid. If none of the categories comes to five awsuq, then there is no zakat to pay. The explanation of this is that when a man harvests five awsuq of dates (from his palms), he adds them all together and deducts the zakat from them even if they are all of different kinds and varieties. It is the same with different kinds of cereal, such as brown wheat, white wheat, barley and sult, which are all considered as one category. If a man reaps five awsuq of any of these, he adds it all together and pays zakat on it. If it does not come to that amount he does not have to pay any zakat. It is the same (also) with grapes, whether they be black or red. If a man picks five awsuq of them he has to pay zakat on them, but if they do not come to that amount he does not have to pay any zakat. Pulses also are considered as one category, like cereals, dates and grapes, even if they are of different varieties and are called by different names. Pulses include chick- peas, lentils, beans, peas, and anything which is agreed by everybody to be a pulse. If a man harvests five awsuq of pulses, measuring by the aforementioned sa, the sa of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, he collects them all together and must pay zakat on them, even if they are of every kind of pulse and not just one kind.&quot; Malik said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab drew a distinction between pulses and wheat when he took zakat from the Nabatean christians. He considered all pulses to be one category and took a tenth from them, and from cereals and raisins he took a twentieth.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If some one asks, &#x27;How can pulses be added up all together when assessing the zakat so that there is just one payment, when a man can barter two of one kind for one of another, while cereals can not be bartered at a rate of two to one?&#x27;, then tell him, &#x27;Gold and silver are collected together when assessing the zakat, even though an amount of gold dinars can be exchanged for many times tha tamount of silver dirhams.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, regarding date palms which are shared equally between two men, and from which eight awsuq of dates are harvested, &quot;They do not have to pay any zakat on them. If one man owns five awsuq of what is harvested from one piece of land, and the other owns four awsuq or less, the one who owns the five awsuq has to pay zakat, and the other one, who harvested four awsuq or less, does not have to pay zakat. This is how things are done whenever there are associates in any crop, whether the crop is grain or seeds that are reaped, or dates that are harvested, or grapes that are picked . Any one of them that harvests five awsuq of dates, or picks five awsuq of grapes, or reaps five awsuq of wheat, has to pay zakat, and whoever&#x27;s portion is less than five awsuq does not have to pay zakat. Zakat only has to be paid by someone whose harvesting or picking or reaping comes to five awsuq.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The sunna with us regarding anything from any of these categories, i.e. wheat, dates, grapes and any kind of grain o rseed, which has had the zakat deducted from it and is then stored by its owner for a number of years after he has paid the zakat on it until he sell sit, is that he does not have to pay any zakat on the price he sells it for until a year has elapsed over it from the day he made the sale, as long as he got it through (chance) acquisition or some other means and it was not intended for trading. Cereals, seeds and trade-goods are the same, in that if a man acquires some and keeps them for a number of years and then sells them for gold or silver, he does not have to pay zakat on their price until a year has elapsed over it from the day of sale. If, however, the goods were intended for trade then the owner must pay zakat on them when he sells them, as long as he has had them for a year from the day when he paid zakat on the property with which he bought them.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The sunna that we are all agreed upon here (in Madina) and which I have heard from the people of knowledge, is that there is no zakat on any kind of fresh (soft) fruit, whether it be pomegranates, peaches, figs or anything that is like them or not like them as long as it is fruit.&quot; He continued, &quot;No zakat has to be paid on animal fodder or herbs and vegetables of any kind, and there is no zakat to pay on the price realised on their sale until a year has elapsed over it from the day of sale which counts as the time the owner receives the sum.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Umar from Sulayman ibn Yasar from Irak ibn Malik from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;A muslim does not have to pay any zakat on his slave or his horse</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 611]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/611" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 610

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A612)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 612]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/612" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 612</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   612 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ أَهْلَ الشَّامِ، قَالُوا لأَبِي عُبَيْدَةَ بْنِ الْجَرَّاحِ خُذْ مِنْ خَيْلِنَا وَرَقِيقِنَا صَدَقَةً ‏.‏ فَأَبَى ثُمَّ كَتَبَ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَأَبَى عُمَرُ ثُمَّ كَلَّمُوهُ أَيْضًا فَكَتَبَ إِلَى عُمَرَ فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ إِنْ أَحَبُّوا فَخُذْهَا مِنْهُمْ وَارْدُدْهَا عَلَيْهِمْ وَارْزُقْ رَقِيقَهُمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَعْنَى قَوْلِهِ رَحِمَهُ اللَّهُ وَارْدُدْهَا عَلَيْهِمْ يَقُولُ عَلَى فُقَرَائِهِمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/612" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Ubayda ibn al-Jarrah (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Sulayman ibn Yasar that the people of Syria said to Abu Ubayda ibn al-Jarrah, &quot;Take zakat from our horses and slaves,&quot; and he refused. Then he wrote to Umar ibn al-Khattab and he (also) refused. Again they talked to him and again he wrote to Umar, and Umar wrote back to him saying, &quot;If they want, take it from them and (then) give it back to them and give their slaves provision.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 612]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/612" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 612</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   612 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What he means, may Allah have mercy upon him, by the words &#x27;and give it back to them&#x27; is, &#x27;to their poor</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/612" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुलईमान बिन यसार से रिवायत है के शाम के लोगों ने अबू उबैदाह बिन जर्राह से कहा के हमारे घोड़ों और गुलामों की ज़कात लिया करो, उन्होंने इनकार किया और हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) को लिख भेजा। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने भी इनकार किया। फिर लोगों ने दोबारा अबू उबैदाह से कहा, उन्होंने हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) को लिखा। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने जवाब में लिखा के अगर वो लोग इन चीज़ों की ज़कात देना चाहें तो उसे उनसे ले कर उन्हीं के फकीरों को दे दे और उनके गुलामों और लौंडियों की खुराक में सर्फ कर।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/612" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سلیمان بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ شام کے لوگوں نے ابوعبیدہ بن جراح سے کہا کہ ہمارے گھوڑوں اور غلاموں کی زکوة لیا کرو انہوں نے انکار کیا اور حضرت عمر بن خطاب کو لکھ بھیجا حضرت عمر نے بھی انکار کیا پھر لوگوں نے دوبارہ ابوعبیدہ سے کہا انہوں نے حضرت عمر کو لکھا حضرت عمر نے جواب میں لکھا کہ اگر وہ لوگ ان چیزوں کی زکوة دینا چاہیں تو اسے ان سے لے کر انہی کے فقیروں کو دے دے اور ان کے غلاموں اور لونڈیوں کی خوراک میں صرف کر ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sulaiman bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke shaam ke logon ne Abu Ubaidah bin Jarrah se kaha ke hamare ghodon aur ghulamon ki zakat liya karo, unhone inkar kiya aur Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko likh bheja. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne bhi inkar kiya. Phir logon ne dobara Abu Ubaidah se kaha, unhone Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko likha. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne jawab mein likha ke agar woh log in cheezon ki zakat dena chahein to usay unse le kar unhi ke faqiron ko de de aur unke ghulamon aur londiyon ki khorak mein sarf kar.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/612" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 611

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 613</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ عَمْرِو بْنِ حَزْمٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَ كِتَابٌ مِنْ عُمَرَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ إِلَى أَبِي وَهُوَ بِمِنًى أَنْ لاَ يَأْخُذَ مِنَ الْعَسَلِ وَلاَ مِنَ الْخَيْلِ صَدَقَةً ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/613" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr ibn Amr ibn Hazim said, &quot;A message came from Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz to my father when he was in Mina telling him not to take zakat from either honey or horses</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 613]</p>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ دِينَارٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سَأَلْتُ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ عَنْ صَدَقَةِ الْبَرَاذِينِ، فَقَالَ وَهَلْ فِي الْخَيْلِ مِنْ صَدَقَةٍ</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abdullah ibn Dinar said, &quot;I asked Said ibn al-Musayyab about zakat on work-horses, and he said, &#x27;Is there any zakat on horses ?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 614]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/614" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 613

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A615)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، قَالَ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَخَذَ الْجِزْيَةَ مِنْ مَجُوسِ الْبَحْرَيْنِ وَأَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ أَخَذَهَا مِنْ مَجُوسِ فَارِسَ وَأَنَّ عُثْمَانَ بْنَ عَفَّانَ أَخَذَهَا مِنَ الْبَرْبَرِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 615]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/615" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 615</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   615 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Ibn Shihab said, &quot;I have heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, took jizya from the magians of Bahrain, that Umar ibn al- Khattab took it from the magians of Persia and that Uthman ibn Affan took it from the Berbers</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 615]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/615" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शिहाब से रिवायत है के मुझे खबर पहुंची के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने बहरीन के मजूसी (फायर-वर्शिपर्स) से जज़िया लिया, और उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फ़ारस के मजूसी से जज़िया लिया, और उस्मान बिन अफ्फान (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने बरबर से जज़िया लिया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 615]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/615" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ پہنچا مجھ کو کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جزیہ لیا بحرین کے مجوس سے اور عمر بن خطاب نے جزیہ لیا فارس کے مجوس سے اور عثمان بن عفان نے جزیہ لیا بربر سے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 615]</p>

## Narration 614

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A616)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 17</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ جَعْفَرِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَلِيٍّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، ذَكَرَ الْمَجُوسَ فَقَالَ مَا أَدْرِي كَيْفَ أَصْنَعُ فِي أَمْرِهِمْ فَقَالَ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ عَوْفٍ أَشْهَدُ لَسَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ سُنُّوا بِهِمْ سُنَّةَ أَهْلِ الْكِتَابِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 616]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/616" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 616</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   616 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Jafar ibn Muhammad ibn Ali from his father that Umar ibn al-Khattab mentioned the magians and said, &quot;I do not know what to do about them.&quot; Abd ar-Rahman ibn Awf said, &quot;I bear witness that I heard the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, say, &#x27;Follow the same sunna with them that you follow with the people of the Book</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 616]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/616" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 616</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   616 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मुहम्मद बिन बाक़िर से रिवायत है के हज़रात उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने मजूस का ज़िक्र किया और कहा के मैं नहीं जानता क्या करूँ उनके बारे में. तो अब्दुर रहमान बिन ऑफ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: मैं गवाही देता हूँ के मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को फ़रमाते सुना कि उनके साथ वही तरीक़ा बर्टो जो अहले किताब के साथ बरते हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 616]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/616" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">امام محمد بن باقر سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر بن خطاب نے ذکر کیا مجوس کا اور کہا کہ میں نہیں جانتا کیا کروں ان کے بارے میں تو کہا عبدالرحمن بن عوف نے گواہی دیتا ہوں میں کہ سنا میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے فرماتے تھے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ان سے وہ طریقہ برتو جو اہل کتاب سے برتتے ہو ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 616]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/616" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Muhammad bin Baqir se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne majoos ka zikr kiya aur kaha ke main nahi janta kya karun unke bare mein. To Abdur Rahman bin Auf (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Main gawahi deta hoon ke maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko farmate suna ke unke sath wahi tareeqa barto jo Ahle Kitab ke sath barte ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 616]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ أَسْلَمَ، مَوْلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، ضَرَبَ الْجِزْيَةَ عَلَى أَهْلِ الذَّهَبِ أَرْبَعَةَ دَنَانِيرَ وَعَلَى أَهْلِ الْوَرِقِ أَرْبَعِينَ دِرْهَمًا مَعَ ذَلِكَ أَرْزَاقُ الْمُسْلِمِينَ وَضِيَافَةُ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Aslam, the mawla of Umar ibn al-Khattab, that Umar ibn al-Khattab imposed a jizya tax of four dinars on those living where gold was the currency, and forty dirhams on those living where silver was the currency. In addition, they had to provide for the muslims and receive them as guests for three days</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 617]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/617" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">असलम, जो मौला हैं उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) के, से रिवायत है के उमर बिन खत्ताब (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने सोना रखने वालों पर हर साल चार दीनार और चांदी रखने वालों पर हर साल चालीस दिरहम जज़िया मुक़र्रर किया और साथ ही यह भी शर्त थी के भूके मुसलमानों को खाना खिलायें और जो कोई मुसलमान उनके यहां आकर उतरे तो उसकी तीन रोज़ तक ज़ियाफत (मेहमानदारी) करें।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/617" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">اسلم جو مولیٰ ہیں عمر بن خطاب کے سے روایت ہے کہ عمر بن خطاب نے مقرر کیا جزیہ کو سونے والوں پر ہر سال میں چار دینار اور چاندی والوں پر ہر سال میں چالیس درہم اور ساتھ اس کے یہ بھی تھا کہ بھوکے مسلمانوں کو کھانا کھلائیں اور جو کوئی مسلمان ان کے یہاں آکر اترے تو اس کی تین روز کی ضیافت کریں ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Aslam, jo maula hain Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke, se riwayat hai ke Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne sona rakhne walon par har saal char dinar aur chandi rakhne walon par har saal chalis dirham jizya muqarrar kiya aur sath hi yeh bhi shart thi ke bhooke musalmanon ko khana khilayen aur jo koi musalman unke yahan akar utray to uski teen roz tak ziyafat (mehmandari) karein.</p>

<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ لِعُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ إِنَّ فِي الظَّهْرِ نَاقَةً عَمْيَاءَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ ادْفَعْهَا إِلَى أَهْلِ بَيْتٍ يَنْتَفِعُونَ بِهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ وَهِيَ عَمْيَاءُ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ يَقْطُرُونَهَا بِالإِبِلِ ‏.‏ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ كَيْفَ تَأْكُلُ مِنَ الأَرْضِ قَالَ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ أَمِنْ نَعَمِ الْجِزْيَةِ هِيَ أَمْ مِنْ نَعَمِ الصَّدَقَةِ فَقُلْتُ بَلْ مِنْ نَعَمِ الْجِزْيَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ عُمَرُ أَرَدْتُمْ - وَاللَّهِ - أَكْلَهَا ‏.‏ فَقُلْتُ إِنَّ عَلَيْهَا وَسْمَ الْجِزْيَةِ ‏.‏ فَأَمَرَ بِهَا عُمَرُ فَنُحِرَتْ وَكَانَ عِنْدَهُ صِحَافٌ تِسْعٌ فَلاَ تَكُونُ فَاكِهَةٌ وَلاَ طُرَيْفَةٌ إِلاَّ جَعَلَ مِنْهَا فِي تِلْكَ الصِّحَافِ فَبَعَثَ بِهَا إِلَى أَزْوَاجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَيَكُونُ الَّذِي يَبْعَثُ بِهِ إِلَى حَفْصَةَ ابْنَتِهِ مِنْ آخِرِ ذَلِكَ فَإِنْ كَانَ فِيهِ نُقْصَانٌ كَانَ فِي حَظِّ حَفْصَةَ - قَالَ - فَجَعَلَ فِي تِلْكَ الصِّحَافِ مِنْ لَحْمِ تِلْكَ الْجَزُورِ فَبَعَثَ بِهِ إِلَى أَزْوَاجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأَمَرَ بِمَا بَقِيَ مِنْ لَحْمِ تِلْكَ الْجَزُورِ فَصُنِعَ فَدَعَا عَلَيْهِ الْمُهَاجِرِينَ وَالأَنْصَارَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ أَرَى أَنْ تُؤْخَذَ النَّعَمُ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْجِزْيَةِ إِلاَّ فِي جِزْيَتِهِمْ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/618" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from his father that he said to Umar ibn al-Khattab, &quot;There is a blind she- camel behind the house,&#x27;&#x27; soUmar said, &quot;Hand it over to a household so that they can make (some) use of it.&quot; He said, &quot;But she is blind.&quot; Umar replied, &quot;Then put it in a line with other camels.&quot; He said, &quot;How will it be able to eat from the ground?&quot; Umar asked, &quot;Is it from the livestock of the jizya or the zakat?&quot; and Aslam replied, &quot;From the livestock of the jizya.&quot; Umar said, &quot;By AIIah, you wish to eat it.&quot; Aslam said, &quot;It has the brand of the jizya on it.&quot; So Umar ordered it to be slaughtered. He had nine platters, and on each of the platters he put some of every fruit and delicacy that there was and then sent them to the wives of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and the one he sent to his daughter Hafsa was the last of them all, and if there was any deficiency in any of them it was in Hafsa&#x27;s portion. &quot;He put meat from the slaughtered animal on the platters and sent them to the wives of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and he ordered what was left of the meat of the slaughtered animal to be prepared. Then he invited the Muhajirun and the Ansar to eat it.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 618]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/618" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 618]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/618" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 618</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   618 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">असलम बिन अद्वि से रिवायत है के उन्होंने हज़रात उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से कहा के शूटर-खाने (कैमल पेन) में एक अंधी ऊँटनी है. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने फ़रमाया: वह ऊँटनी किसी घर वालों को दे दो ताकि वह उससे नफा उठायें. मैंने कहा: वह अंधी है. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: उसको ऊंटों की क़तर में बांध देंगे. मैंने कहा: वह चारा कैसे खाये गई? हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: वह जिज़ये के जानवरों में से है या सदक़ह के. मैंने कहा: जिज़ये के. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: वल्लाह तुम लोगों ने उसके खाने का इरादा किया है. मैंने कहा: नहीं नहर (ज़िबह) कर दी गयी. हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास नौ पियले थे रख कर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की बीबियों को भेजा करते और सब से आखिर में अपनी बेटी हफ्सा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) के पास भेजते. अगर वह चीज़ कम होती तो कभी हफ्सा के हिस्से में होती. तो पहले आप ने गोश्त को पियालों में डाल कर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की बीबियों को रवाना किया और सब मुहाजिरीन और अन्सार की दावत कर दी. इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) को पहूंचा के उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने अपने आमिलों को लिख भेजा कि जो लोग जिज़्या देने वालों में से मुसलमान हो जाएँ</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/618" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">اسلم بن عدوی سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے کہا حضرت عمر بن خطاب سے کہ شتر خانے میں ایک اندھی اونٹنی ہے تو فرمایا حضرت عمر نے وہ اونٹنی کسی گھروالوں کو دے دے تاکہ وہ اس سے نفع اٹھائیں میں نے کہا وہ اندھی ہے حضرت عمر نے کہا اس کو اونٹوں کی قطار میں باندھ دیں گے میں نے کہا وہ چارہ کیسے کھائے گی حضرت عمر نے کہا وہ جزیے کے جانوروں میں سے ہے یا صدقہ کے میں نے کہا وہ جزیے کے حضرت عمر نے کہا و اللہ تم لوگوں نے اس کے کھانے کا ارادہ کیا ہے میں نے کہا نہیں اس پر نشانی جزیہ کی موجود ہے تو حکم کیا حضرت عمر نے اور وہ نحر کی گئی اور حضرت عمر کے پاس نو پیالے تھے جو میوہ یا اچھی چیز آتی آپ ان میں رکھ کر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی بیبیوں کو بھیجا کرتے اور سب سے آخر میں اپنی بیٹی حفصہ کے پاس بھیجتے اگر وہ چیز کم ہوتی تو کمی حفصہ کے حصے میں ہوتی تو پہلے آپ نے گوشت کو پیالوں میں ڈال کر آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی بیبیوں کو روانہ کیا بعد اس کے پکانے کا حکم کیا اور سب مہاجرین اور انصار کی دعوت کر دی ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا کہ عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے لکھ بھیجا اپنے عاملوں کو جو لوگ جزیہ والوں میں سے مسلمان ہوں ان کا جزیہ معاف کریں ۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 618</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   618 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Aslam bin Adwi se riwayat hai ke unhone Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) se kaha ke shutar-khane (camel pen) mein ek andhi oontni hai. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne farmaya: Woh oontni kisi ghar walon ko de do taake woh usse nafa uthayen. Maine kaha: Woh andhi hai. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Usko oonton ki qatar mein bandh denge. Maine kaha: Woh chara kaise khaye gi? Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Woh jizye ke janwaron mein se hai ya sadaqah ke. Maine kaha: Jizye ke. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Wallah tum logon ne uske khane ka irada kiya hai. Maine kaha: Nahin, us par nishani jizya ki maujood hai. Phir Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne hukm diya aur woh nahr (zibah) kar di gayi. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas nau piyale thay, jo mewa ya achi cheez aati aap un mein rakh kar Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki bibiyon ko bheja karte aur sab se aakhir mein apni beti Hafsa (Radi Allahu Anha) ke paas bhejte. Agar woh cheez kam hoti to kami Hafsa ke hisse mein hoti. To pehle aap ne gosht ko piyalon mein daal kar Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki bibiyon ko rawana kiya, baad iske pakane ka hukm diya aur sab muhajireen aur ansar ki dawat kar di. Imam Malik (Rahmatullah Alaih) ko pahuncha ke Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne apne amilon ko likh bheja ke jo log jizya dene walon mein se musalman ho jayen, unka jizya maaf kar dein.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/618" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، كَتَبَ إِلَى عُمَّالِهِ أَنْ يَضَعُوا الْجِزْيَةَ عَمَّنْ أَسْلَمَ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْجِزْيَةِ حِينَ يُسْلِمُونَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ مَضَتِ السُّنَّةُ أَنْ لاَ جِزْيَةَ عَلَى نِسَاءِ أَهْلِ الْكِتَابِ وَلاَ عَلَى صِبْيَانِهِمْ وَأَنَّ الْجِزْيَةَ لاَ تُؤْخَذُ إِلاَّ مِنَ الرِّجَالِ الَّذِينَ قَدْ بَلَغُوا الْحُلُمَ وَلَيْسَ عَلَى أَهْلِ الذِّمَّةِ وَلاَ عَلَى الْمَجُوسِ فِي نَخِيلِهِمْ وَلاَ كُرُومِهِمْ وَلاَ زُرُوعِهِمْ وَلاَ مَوَاشِيهِمْ صَدَقَةٌ لأَنَّ الصَّدَقَةَ إِنَّمَا وُضِعَتْ عَلَى الْمُسْلِمِينَ تَطْهِيرًا لَهُمْ وَرَدًّا عَلَى فُقَرَائِهِمْ وَوُضِعَتِ الْجِزْيَةُ عَلَى أَهْلِ الْكِتَابِ صَغَارًا لَهُمْ فَهُمْ مَا كَانُوا بِبَلَدِهِمُ الَّذِينَ صَالَحُوا عَلَيْهِ لَيْسَ عَلَيْهِمْ شَىْءٌ سِوَى الْجِزْيَةِ فِي شَىْءٍ مِنْ أَمْوَالِهِمْ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَتَّجِرُوا فِي بِلاَدِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ وَيَخْتَلِفُوا فِيهَا فَيُؤْخَذُ مِنْهُمُ الْعُشْرُ فِيمَا يُدِيرُونَ مِنَ التِّجَارَاتِ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهُمْ إِنَّمَا وُضِعَتْ عَلَيْهِمُ الْجِزْيَةُ وَصَالَحُوا عَلَيْهَا عَلَى أَنْ يُقَرُّوا بِبِلاَدِهِمْ وَيُقَاتَلَ عَنْهُمْ عَدُوُّهُمْ فَمَنْ خَرَجَ مِنْهُمْ مِنْ بِلاَدِهِ إِلَى غَيْرِهَا يَتْجُرُ إِلَيْهَا فَعَلَيْهِ الْعُشْرُ مَنْ تَجَرَ مِنْهُمْ مِنْ أَهْلِ مِصْرَ إِلَى الشَّامِ وَمِنْ أَهْلِ الشَّامِ إِلَى الْعِرَاقِ وَمِنْ أَهْلِ الْعِرَاقِ إِلَى الْمَدِينَةِ أَوِ الْيَمَنِ أَوْ مَا أَشْبَهَ هَذَا مِنَ الْبِلاَدِ فَعَلَيْهِ الْعُشْرُ وَلاَ صَدَقَةَ عَلَى أَهْلِ الْكِتَابِ وَلاَ الْمَجُوسِ فِي شَىْءٍ مِنْ أَمْوَالِهِمْ وَلاَ مِنْ مَوَاشِيهِمْ وَلاَ ثِمَارِهِمْ وَلاَ زُرُوعِهِمْ مَضَتْ بِذَلِكَ السُّنَّةُ وَيُقَرُّونَ عَلَى دِينِهِمْ وَيَكُونُونَ عَلَى مَا كَانُوا عَلَيْهِ وَإِنِ اخْتَلَفُوا فِي الْعَامِ الْوَاحِدِ مِرَارًا فِي بِلاَدِ الْمُسْلِمِينَ فَعَلَيْهِمْ كُلَّمَا اخْتَلَفُوا الْعُشْرُ لأَنَّ ذَلِكَ لَيْسَ مِمَّا صَالَحُوا عَلَيْهِ وَلاَ مِمَّا شُرِطَ لَهُمْ وَهَذَا الَّذِي أَدْرَكْتُ عَلَيْهِ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا ‏.‏</p>
